  "Ka fito ya Haider kar abincin ka yayi sanyi" ta fita daga dakin da 😃a fuskarta, beh ji dadin yadda bata aminta dashi bah, bayan shi duka wani sirri da abubuwa masu mahimmanci shi ita ya bah ta ajemai, haka weekend din ya kare daya shigo dakin zata kashe 💻 din ta aje in aiki takeyi dashi, pad da wayarta mah duk akwai password abinda da babu dan haka hankalinshi yaki kwanciya da ita.

"Safwana meh kikeyi anan" yayi mamakin ganinta a office dinshi 
  "Kayi hakuri ban dauki izinin ka bah na fito, ina son in baka surprise ne shi yasa" ya aje pen dinshi saman table yana kallonta
  "Ban manta bah ka taba cewa in kawo maka breakfast office mah whole week" ta daura wani picnic basket saman table din dake tsakiyar office din ba wanda yakeyin aiki a sama bah
  "Haba wasa nakeyi ai Safwana ba da gaske nakeyi bah" ya fada duk da center table din yake nufa ya bude basket din
  "Hmmmmmm, naji kanshi na tashi" ya fiddo komai ya jera saman table din
  "Bari inje inga yadda ayyuka ke tafiya kafin ka gama" ya girgixa mata kai
  "Aa zaki zauna ki tayani ci ne" taje kusa dashi ta zauna
  "Kasan yau ba ranar girkina bane bah, kuma naji baka karya bah ka fito shi yasa na kawo maka, in shaa Allah in na koma zan fadama Hau'wa dan karta ga kaman munafurtar ta mukeyi, in tace bataso bazan kara kawo maka bah" 
  "Okay" ya jin jina kai
"Kuma dan Allah ki tafi gda daga nan banda biyawa ko ina" tayi 😃
  "In shaa Allah yaya Haider, sai ka dawo Allah ya bada sa'a" har bayan ta tafi yana cin abincin murmushi yakeyi na jin dadi dan yanxu da kanta ta kulashi, sai bayan dan lokaci kuma yayi ma ksnshi fada da tunatarwa akan dalin aurensu, ita kuma dama taxo ne dan ta samu tayi magana da masu kula da accounts din company din, domin ta basu details din abinda zasu bukata wajen biyan bashin da ake bin company din
   "Safwana har yanxu baki tafi bah" tana fitowa daga elevator sukaci karo shi kuma ze shiga wani meeting 
  "Dama yanxu zan tafi" ya kalli ⌚ hannunshi yaga dadewar datayi a office din
   "Muje in kaiki gda" ta bude baki zata hana secretary dinshi ta rigata magana
  "Sir, bazamu iya adjusting meeting din nan bah, kuma its important" yayi ajiyar zuciya itama tayi, abinda ya kara bashi mamaki dan ya lura da relief din daya bayyana a fuskarta
  "Cancel the meeting, gda zan kaiki"
"Bana so bana bukata" ta fada tana gudu gudu sauri sauri ta koma cikin elevator din, wanda ya hana rufewa da hannun shi sannan ya shiga shima, 
  "Kaini gda zakayi dan ka tabbatar gda zanje,yadda dani ne bakayi bah kuma" ta tambaya rakube can baya shi kuma ya tsare a gaba
   "Ina so in tabbatar kinje gda lafiya, dan in samu kwanciyar hankali" suka kalli juna a jikin elevator din da yake silver Colour ne
  "Basai ka nuna kaman ka damu dani bah, zan iya kula da kaina, in wani yaga yadda kake san sanin inda nake kowani lokaci sai yace so da kishina kakeyi" ta dage kai tana kallon gefe
  "Kulawa nake baki dan ya zama responsibility dina bawai so bane bah, balle mah wai kishi" ya cije hakora
  "Uhm hmmm" 😤 haushi ya kara kamashi
  "Ina so nan da minti 30 ki turomin pic dinki wanda zaki dauka in kin kai gda" ya latsa hawan da suke sannan ya sauka, daze fita ya juya yaga bashi take kallo bah, dan haka ya koma meeting din shi, 
  ^√Ban gani bah har yanxu√^ ji tayi kaman ta kwala 📱din a kasa saboda haushi daya kara kamata, ita ta gaji gwara su san abinyi game da uncle koh wannan zaman ze zo karshe, tun kafin sanshi yayi mata tabo a zuciya
  ^√Gashi nan, happy 😄√^ ta tura mai hotonta data dauka gaban madubi da waya yana kare mata fuska
  ^√Hotonki nace ina so√^kayi meh dashi ta tambayi kanta, harta yi niyyar dauka kuma ta aje wayar dan karta fassara hakan a matsayin son ganinta yakeyi
  "Sir?" 
"Uhm" ya dago yaga duk sun zubamai idanu, mikewa yayi yace sai sun sake taruwa yanxu suje suyi duk abubuwan da aka fada kuma ya yadda ayi
  ^√Am waiting√^ ya tura mata dan ya dan ji dadin chat din nasu
   ^√Baka gani ya kamata ace mun yarda akan abubuwan da bazamu yi bah acikin auren nan,ni ina ganin wannan abin mah ya kamata ya shiga ciki√^   yayi tsaki a fili daya sa mutane juyowa su kalleshi, tashi yayi ya fita yana harbin iska da tafiyar shi 'ita wai meh yasa take san tunamin cewa aurenmu bana dindin din bane bah, kaman ban sani bah koh gani takeyi ban isa ta zauna dani har karshen rayuwarta bah' ya shiga office dinshi ya zauna saman kujera yana jujjuyawa
  ^√Meye dalilin ki na fadan haka, koh kina tsoron zaki fara sona ne√^ yayi murmushin mugunta dan ya hango yadda fuskarta zatayi, amma bata wani bata rai koh tayi looking surprised bah sai sad datayi feeling 
   ^√Karka damu in akwai abu daya dana yi mah kaina alkwarin bazanyi bah acikin auren nan shine sanka√^ ya rasa abinda yasa yaji ba dadi kuma farin cikinshi ya tafi lokaci daya, beep yaji alamun wani text ya sake shigowa da sauri ya jawo 📱har wayan na faduwa, bayan ya dauko ne yayi ajiyar zuciya sannan ya duba
  ^√Kai nake jiye mawah dan karka fara sona in pics dina suka cika wayarka,duk da haka zan tausaya in turo maka one,P.Skarka rude ka kasa aiki in ka gani 😘 √^ pic daya ta turomai amma ya tsura ido yana kallo yafi minti goma yana abu daya, daga gani zaune take saman gado dan ga pillows kana gani a background, batada dankwali a kanta gashinta kuma gefe tayi dashi ya zuba har saman kirjinta, ta cire kayan datazo office dasu, yanzu wata rigace ja da zip a gaba mai karamin hannu, ta dauki hoton ne tana turo lips 💋 da hannunta saman zip din ta dan ja beh kai karshe bah amma kana ganin saman dukiyar fulaninta  da lace din white bra dinta daya fata fitowa, bayan ya kare ma hoton kallo ya aje wayar a hankali saman table sannan ya shafa fuskarshi da ✋,kaman yace ta kara turomai wasu pics kuma sai ya fasa, gallery dinshi ya shiga dan ya zabi pic dinshi daya yayi collage dinsu tare, 
  "What, oh my God ina da wasu pics dinta ashe" na bikin sune wanda Khadija ta turomai wasu kuma shi yayi screenshot da kanshi, na ranar kamu tasha yellow material riga da skirt meh kyalkyali da dark blue yan kunne da sarka sai mayafi meh dan girma, akwai wani data dauka da gown baki meh zane ja ajikinshi na flowers kana na, sannan tayi rolling da ligh brown dayan color din dake jikin gown din, koh ba a fada bah yasan na ranar walima ne, na daurin aure shine tasa shadda atamfa fari da coffee color sai mayafi fari meh duwatsu ajikinshi da wasu flat silver stones a jiki, ajiyar zuciya yayi dayaga nxt din, lace ne ash complete harda decorations din jiki, zani ne akayi mata data daura saman rigan sai wani chain black color data dora daidai inda ta daura zanin yasan wannan duk aikin Khadija ne dan make up din kanshi na daukan ido ne, head din da aka daura mata ash ne da kwalliya silver sai yan kunne da sarka silver meh beads baki ajiki, atamfa pink meh haske da yellow shima meh haske sosai ya ganta nxt, dama can yasan tana san colors masu haske tun daga yadda take decorating dakinta, a takaice mah dai beh kara wani aiki bah sai kallon hotunanta kuma na ita kadai yakeson gani, in yazo na wanda tayi da friends koh family sai ya wuce, wanda tayi da yan office dinsu ne mah ya dan kalla, 
   "Tare zamu tafi dan tani mah ya tafi da dayar matarshi" ya zauna saman gado yana kallonta tana hada kaya
"Saboda jabbar da hameed ne baxe tafi da ita bah koh" ya fadama mom dinshi wacce ta jin jina mai kai
  "Kuma mah ai ke suka sani basu san aunty jameela bah, kedai din ya kamata kiji" wani abokin babanshi ne ya rasu, yana dawowa ya iske mamanshi na shiri
  "Da anyi sadakar uku zamu dawo, haka yace ina ga saboda ayyukanshi ne, duk da naga ya daina fita kwata kwata kullum yana gda" abin ya dauremai kai amma beh ba hakan wani mahimmanci bah
  "Yayi gabane shi"
"Eh, yanxu zanje in iske shi, ka kula da kanka kafin in dawo" yayi murmushi
  "Mom ni din kaman wani yarone da zaki wani damu" itama tayi murmushi 
  "Kai kadai ne yarona" ta dafa kanahi
"Harsai ka haifi naka sannan in daukesu su zama nawa" ta fada da murna da murmushi a fuskarta shi kuma gabanshi ne ya fadi daya tuno da rabon kila ya gama rayuwarshi shima beh da dan dazece nashi ne, bayan ya kaita airport ya dawo ya zauna saman gado yana tunani, hannu kawai yaji saman wuyanshi yayi firgigit
  "Baki shigo da sallama bane bah?" Ya fada yana matsawa
  "Nayi sallama manah" ta turo baki
"Kai ne dai bakaji bah, ina fata tunani na kakeyi" ta dan rugumoshi kuma ta matsa gefe
  "Ya kamata kayi wanka manah tunda daga office ka dawo sannan ka kwanta" ta tashi daga kusa dashi, beh bita dakinta bah bayan ya fito wanka itama kuma bata dawo bah, maganin asibiti dana gda wanda yakesha ya jawo yana kalo sannan yasha su dan beh taba tsallakewa bah, a ka'idarsu yake shan su,  ya jin gina da gado kaman marar lfy yana karatun alkur'ani, da dare yayi tsakiya kuma ya tashi yana ibada dan neman rabo daga madaukakin sarki. 
  "Baki sake zuwa office din bah kuma" kaman yadda yace ne beh sake ganinta bah saida ranar girkinta ya zagayo
  "Kaga na manta mah ban fada mah Hau'wa bah, ba matsala in ka fadamata da kanka koh" ta dauki kayan baccin data aje saman gado wanda zatasa in ta fito wanka ta shiga dasu bayin, tashi zaune yayi dan gama cin abincin su kenan ya hau gadogado, zagaye dakin ya fara yana buga 🎮 a iPad  dinta wanda ta cire mah password, bata cire na 📱 da 💻bah dan saida ya duba
  "Meye hakan kakeyi" ta shigo da towel tana goge ruwan kanta da fuska
"Dan kar in kwanta ne daga cin abinci" ya fada da 😃
  "Okay" gaban madubi taje ta shafa turarenta da man shafawa da dare ta nannade 💇 cikin towel
  "Har lokacin baccin yayi" ta daga mai kai kawai ta kwanta
  "Yaya Haider ka kashe fitila pls" yaje ya kashe fitilan itama ta kashe nata na kusa da gado, kwanciya yayi ba tareda ya kashe nashi side lamp din bah dan yana san suna ganin fuskokin juna lokacin da suke raya sunna
  "Safwana" ya rada cikin kunnenta
"Uhmmm" ta fada da 😪 na karya
   "Abinda kikayi gudu ya faru" ta juya tana kallonshi
"Tun shekaranjiya nake kaguwa yau tazo, nake mafarkin ki Safwana" 😨, dariya kallon datayi mai ya bashi
  "Da gaske nakeyi, kuma yanxu da ranar tazo saiki juyamin baya" abin nan kara daure mata kai yakeyi, saita daina rabarshi kuma sai yazo yana fadamata kalamai irin wadan nan sannan kuma yace baya santa, 
  "Basai ka kanainaye ni da kalamai bah ya Haider, karka manta nasan kana da mata wacce kakeso,na tabbata da kuna tare  koh meh kama dani baka tuno bah" 😃 yayi ya shafa fuskarta
  "Kin manta itama ba auren soyayya mukayi bah, har yanxu ban fara santa bah,kaman yadda ban fara sanki bah" yayi yunkurin hada bakinshi da nata sai tayi sairin kauda kai kafin ta gyara kwanciyarta.
